T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 5.0  # Maximum torque in N*m\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Load system configuration\n        with open(self.config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        # System parameters\n        self.EA = config[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = config[\"J\"]\n        self.R = config[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = config[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = config[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = config[\"v0\"]\n        self.dt = config[\"dt\"]\n        self.num_sec = config[\"num_sections\"]\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 2.0  # Maximum torque in N*m (conservative)\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Balanced tension and velocity tracking\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.1] * 6  # Moderate control effort penalty\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control log saved to /root/control_log.json\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from logged data.\"\"\"\n    # Load system config to get references\n    with open('/root/system_config.json', 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    step_time = config[\"step_time\"]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(config[\"T_ref_final\"])\n\n    # Extract data after settling period (start from step_time + 1s)\n    settling_start_time = step_time + 1.0\n    steady_state_data = [entry for entry in log_data if entry[\"time\"] >= settling_start_time]\n\n    if not steady_state_data:\n        steady_state_data = log_data[-100:]  # Load system configuration\n        with open(self.config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        # System parameters\n        self.EA = config[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = config[\"J\"]\n        self.roller_R = config[\"R\"]  # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.roller_R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.roller_R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 2.0  # Maximum torque in N*m (conservative)\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 2.0  # Maximum torque in N*m (conservative)\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Extract data after settling period (start from step_time + 1s)\n    settling_start_time = step_time + 1.0\n    steady_state_data = [entry for entry in log_data if entry[\"time\"] >= settling_start_time]\n\n    if not steady_state_data:\n        steady_state_data = log_data[-100:]  # Compute settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of final reference)\n    settling_time = None\n    tolerance = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n\n    for i, entry in enumerate(log_data):\n        if entry[\"time\"] >= step_time:\n            tensions = np.array(entry[\"tensions\"])\n            ref_tensions = np.array(entry[\"references\"][:6])\n\n            # Check if settled\n            errors = np.abs(tensions - ref_tensions)\n            if np.all(errors <= tolerance):\n                # Check if it stays settled for next 0.5s\n                settled = True\n                check_duration = 0.5\n                check_steps = int(check_duration / config[\"dt\"])\n\n                for j in range(i, min(i + check_steps, len(log_data))):\n                    check_tensions = np.array(log_data[j][\"tensions\"])\n                    check_refs = np.array(log_data[j][\"references\"][:6])\n                    check_errors = np.abs(check_tensions - check_refs)\n                    if not np.all(check_errors <= tolerance):\n                        settled = False\n                        break\n\n                if settled:\n                    settling_time = float(entry[\"time\"] - step_time)\n                    break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = float(log_data[-1][\"time\"] - step_time)\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Balanced tension and velocity tracking\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.1] * 6  # Strong tension tracking\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.01] * 6  # Very low control effort penalty\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMPC Controller for 6-section Roll-to-Roll System\n\nThis module implements Model Predictive Control for tension regulation\nduring reference changes in a Roll-to-Roll manufacturing line.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"MPC Controller with finite-horizon LQR.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/system_config.json\",\n                 controller_params_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller.\"\"\"\n        self.config_path = Path(config_path)\n        self.controller_params_path = Path(controller_params_path)\n\n        # Load system configuration\n        with open(self.config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        # System parameters\n        self.EA = config[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = config[\"J\"]\n        self.roller_R = config[\"R\"]  # Roller radius (renamed to avoid conflict with R matrix)\n        self.fb = config[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = config[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = config[\"v0\"]\n        self.dt = config[\"dt\"]\n        self.num_sec = config[\"num_sections\"]\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.roller_R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.roller_R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.roller_R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 2.0  # Maximum torque in N*m (conservative)\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - balanced approach\n    controller.N = 12\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # Balanced tension and velocity tracking\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.1] * 6  # Moderate control effort penalty\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.roller_R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Load system configuration\n        with open(self.config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        # System parameters\n        self.EA = config[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = config[\"J\"]\n        self.roller_R = config[\"R\"]  # Roller radius (renamed to avoid conflict with R matrix)\n        self.fb = config[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = config[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = config[\"v0\"]\n        self.dt = config[\"dt\"]\n        self.num_sec = config[\"num_sections\"]\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.roller_R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.roller_R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.roller_R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 10.0  # Maximum torque in N*m\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - more aggressive\n    controller.N = 20  # Longer horizon\n    Q_diag = [1000.0] * 6 + [10.0] * 6  # Strong tension tracking\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.01] * 6  # Very low control effort penalty\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Check if settled\n            errors = np.abs(tensions - ref_tensions)\n            if np.all(errors <= tolerance):\n                # Check if it stays settled for next 0.5s\n                settled = True\n                check_duration = 0.5\n                check_steps = int(check_duration / config[\"dt\"])\n\n                for j in range(i, min(i + check_steps, len(log_data))):\n                    check_tensions = np.array(log_data[j][\"tensions\"])\n                    check_refs = np.array(log_data[j][\"references\"][:6])\n                    check_errors = np.abs(check_tensions - check_refs)\n                    if not np.all(check_errors <= tolerance):\n                        settled = False\n                        break\n\n                if settled:\n                    settling_time = float(entry[\"time\"] - step_time)\n                    break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = float(log_data[-1][\"time\"] - step_time)\n\n    # Roller radius (renamed to avoid conflict with R matrix)\n        self.fb = config[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = config[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = config[\"v0\"]\n        self.dt = config[\"dt\"]\n        self.num_sec = config[\"num_sections\"]\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.roller_R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.roller_R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.roller_R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 10.0  # Maximum torque in N*m\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - conservative but effective\n    controller.N = 10\n    Q_diag = [80.0] * 6 + [1.0] * 6  # Tension tracking priority\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.5] * 6  # Higher control effort penalty for stability\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control log saved to /root/control_log.json\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from logged data.\"\"\"\n    # Last 1 second\n\n    # Compute steady-state error (mean absolute error across all tensions)\n    steady_state_errors = []\n    for entry in steady_state_data:\n        tensions = np.array(entry[\"tensions\"])\n        ref_tensions = np.array(entry[\"references\"][:6])\n        error = np.abs(tensions - ref_tensions)\n        steady_state_errors.append(np.mean(error))\n\n    mean_steady_state_error = float(np.mean(steady_state_errors))\n\n    # Load system configuration\n        with open(self.config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        # System parameters\n        self.EA = config[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = config[\"J\"]\n        self.roller_R = config[\"R\"]  # Roller radius (renamed to avoid conflict with R matrix)\n        self.fb = config[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = config[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = config[\"v0\"]\n        self.dt = config[\"dt\"]\n        self.num_sec = config[\"num_sections\"]\n\n        # State and control dimensions\n        self.nx = 2 * self.num_sec  # [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        self.nu = self.num_sec      # [u1..u6]\n\n        # Initial reference point for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n        self.x_ref_op = np.concatenate([self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial])\n        self.u_ref_op = self._compute_u_ref(self.x_ref_op)\n\n        # Derive linearized state-space model\n        self.A, self.B = self._derive_state_space()\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize_system(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Load or initialize controller parameters\n        self._initialize_controller_params()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < self.num_sec - 1 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.roller_R * x_ref[self.num_sec + i] - self.roller_R * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _derive_state_space(self):\n        \"\"\"Derive linearized continuous-time state-space model around operating point.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point\n        T_op = self.T_ref_initial\n        v_op = self.v_ref_initial\n\n        # Initialize A and B matrices\n        A = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nx))\n        B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))\n\n        # Linearize tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        # Partial derivatives:\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1} = v_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i = -v_i/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1} = EA/L + T_{i-1}/L\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i = -EA/L - T_i/L\n\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Get operating point values\n            v_i = v_op[i]\n            T_i = T_op[i]\n            v_prev = v_op[i-1] if i > 0 else self.v0\n            T_prev = T_op[i-1] if i > 0 else 0.0\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = v_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[i, i] = -v_i / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, self.num_sec + i - 1] = self.EA / self.L + T_prev / self.L\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.EA / self.L - T_i / self.L\n\n        # Linearize velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, i] = -self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. T_{i+1}\n            if i < self.num_sec - 1:\n                A[self.num_sec + i, i + 1] = self.roller_R**2 / self.J\n\n            # Partial derivatives w.r.t. v_i\n            A[self.num_sec + i, self.num_sec + i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # Control input effect on v_i\n            B[self.num_sec + i, i] = self.roller_R / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize_system(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using zero-order hold.\"\"\"\n        # Use matrix exponential for better accuracy\n        from scipy.linalg import expm\n\n        # Build augmented matrix for ZOH discretization\n        M = np.zeros((self.nx + self.nu, self.nx + self.nu))\n        M[:self.nx, :self.nx] = A * dt\n        M[:self.nx, self.nx:] = B * dt\n\n        Phi = expm(M)\n\n        Ad = Phi[:self.nx, :self.nx]\n        Bd = Phi[:self.nx, self.nx:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _initialize_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller parameters or load from file.\"\"\"\n        if self.controller_params_path.exists():\n            with open(self.controller_params_path, 'r') as f:\n                params = json.load(f)\n            self.N = params[\"horizon_N\"]\n            self.Q = np.diag(params[\"Q_diag\"])\n            self.R = np.diag(params[\"R_diag\"])\n            self.K_lqr = np.array(params[\"K_lqr\"])\n        else:\n            # Default parameters (will be tuned)\n            self.N = 10\n            # Prioritize tension tracking over velocity tracking\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * self.num_sec + [0.1] * self.num_sec\n            self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.05] * self.nu\n            self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n            self.K_lqr = None\n\n    def solve_lqr(self, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming (Riccati equation).\"\"\"\n        # Terminal cost\n        P = [self.Q.copy()]\n        K = []\n\n        # Backward recursion\n        for k in range(self.N - 1, -1, -1):\n            P_next = P[0]\n\n            # Compute optimal gain with numerical stability\n            BTP = self.Bd.T @ P_next\n            temp = BTP @ self.Bd + self.R\n\n            # Use regularization for numerical stability\n            epsilon = 1e-8\n            temp_reg = temp + epsilon * np.eye(self.nu)\n\n            K_k = np.linalg.solve(temp_reg, BTP @ self.Ad)\n            K.insert(0, K_k)\n\n            # Update cost-to-go\n            ATP = self.Ad.T @ P_next\n            P_k = self.Q + ATP @ self.Ad - ATP @ self.Bd @ K_k\n\n            # Ensure P_k stays symmetric and positive semi-definite\n            P_k = (P_k + P_k.T) / 2\n            P.insert(0, P_k)\n\n        # Store the first gain (for steady-state)\n        self.K_lqr = K[0]\n\n        return K\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute MPC control action.\"\"\"\n        # Solve LQR for current horizon\n        K_sequence = self.solve_lqr(x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Deviation from reference\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_sequence[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        # Saturate control to reasonable limits (prevent extreme torques)\n        u_max = 5.0  # Maximum torque in N*m\n        u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)\n\n        return u\n\n    def save_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Save controller parameters to JSON.\"\"\"\n        params = {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.N),\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist() if self.K_lqr is not None else [],\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n        with open(self.controller_params_path, 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(params, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Controller parameters saved to {self.controller_params_path}\")\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"Run MPC controller through the simulator.\"\"\"\n    from r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n    # Initialize simulator and controller\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    controller = MPCController()\n\n    # Tune controller parameters - conservative but effective\n    controller.N = 10\n    Q_diag = [80.0] * 6 + [1.0] * 6  # Tension tracking priority\n    controller.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R_diag = [0.5] * 6  # Higher control effort penalty for stability\n    controller.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(sim.get_time()),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x = sim.step(u)\n\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller.save_params()\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control log saved to /root/control_log.json\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from logged data.\"\"\"\n    # Load system config to get references\n    with open('/root/system_config.json', 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    step_time = config[\"step_time\"]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(config[\"T_ref_final\"])\n\n    # Extract data after settling period (start from step_time + 1s)\n    settling_start_time = step_time + 1.0\n    steady_state_data = [entry for entry in log_data if entry[\"time\"] >= settling_start_time]\n\n    if not steady_state_data:\n        steady_state_data = log_data[-100:]  # Last 1 second\n\n    # Compute steady-state error (mean absolute error across all tensions)\n    steady_state_errors = []\n    for entry in steady_state_data:\n        tensions = np.array(entry[\"tensions\"])\n        ref_tensions = np.array(entry[\"references\"][:6])\n        error = np.abs(tensions - ref_tensions)\n        steady_state_errors.append(np.mean(error))\n\n    mean_steady_state_error = float(np.mean(steady_state_errors))\n\n    # Compute settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of final reference)\n    settling_time = None\n    tolerance = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n\n    for i, entry in enumerate(log_data):\n        if entry[\"time\"] >= step_time:\n            tensions = np.array(entry[\"tensions\"])\n            ref_tensions = np.array(entry[\"references\"][:6])\n\n            # Check if settled\n            errors = np.abs(tensions - ref_tensions)\n            if np.all(errors <= tolerance):\n                # Check if it stays settled for next 0.5s\n                settled = True\n                check_duration = 0.5\n                check_steps = int(check_duration / config[\"dt\"])\n\n                for j in range(i, min(i + check_steps, len(log_data))):\n                    check_tensions = np.array(log_data[j][\"tensions\"])\n                    check_refs = np.array(log_data[j][\"references\"][:6])\n                    check_errors = np.abs(check_tensions - check_refs)\n                    if not np.all(check_errors <= tolerance):\n                        settled = False\n                        break\n\n                if settled:\n                    settling_time = float(entry[\"time\"] - step_time)\n                    break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = float(log_data[-1][\"time\"] - step_time)\n\n    # Compute max and min tensions across entire simulation\n    all_tensions = [entry[\"tensions\"] for entry in log_data]\n    max_tension = float(np.max(all_tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(all_tensions))\n\n    # Create metrics\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": mean_steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {mean_steady_state_error:.2f}N (target: < 2.0N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s (target: < 4.0s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {max_tension:.2f}N (target: < 50N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {min_tension:.2f}N (target: > 5N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        mean_steady_state_error < 2.0 and\n        settling_time < 4.0 and\n        max_tension < 50.0 and\n        min_tension > 5.0\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nAll targets met: {targets_met}\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"Starting MPC simulation...\")\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    print(\"\\nComputing metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nSimulation complete!\")\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":81,"oldLines":11,"newStart":81,"newLines":12,"lines":["         B = np.zeros((self.nx, self.nu))"," ","         #
